```python
def test_experiment_config_serialization():
    """Test ExperimentConfig serialization round-trip."""
    config = ExperimentConfig(
        name="test_exp",
        description="Test experiment",
        agent_config={"model": "gpt-4"},
        evaluation_config={"layers": [1, 2, 3]},
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        repetition_count=3,
        random_seed=42,
        tags=["test"],
    )

    data = config.to_dict()
    restored = ExperimentConfig.from_dict(data)

    assert restored.name == config.name
    assert restored.description == config.description
    assert restored.agent_config == config.agent_config
    assert restored.repetition_count == config.repetition_count
```

```python
def test_campaign_config_serialization():
    """Test CampaignConfig serialization round-trip."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        description="Test campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", description="Experiment 1"),
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp2", description="Experiment 2"),
        ],
        output_dir="experiments/test",
        comparison_pairs=[("exp1", "exp2")],
        statistical_settings={"confidence_level": 0.95},
    )

    data = campaign.to_dict()
    restored = CampaignConfig.from_dict(data)

    assert restored.campaign_id == campaign.campaign_id
    assert len(restored.experiments) == 2
    assert restored.comparison_pairs == campaign.comparison_pairs
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_campaign_config_hash`  (0.001s)

_Test campaign hash computation._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_config_hash():
    """Test campaign hash computation."""
    campaign1 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", agent_config={"model": "gpt-4"}),
        ],
    )

    campaign2 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", agent_config={"model": "gpt-4"}),
        ],
    )

    # Same config should produce same hash
    assert campaign1.compute_hash() == campaign2.compute_hash()

    # Different config should produce different hash
    campaign3 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", agent_config={"model": "gpt-3.5"}),
        ],
    )

    assert campaign1.compute_hash() != campaign3.compute_hash()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_campaign_runner_simulation`  (0.0232s)

_Test CampaignRunner orchestration with a stubbed _run_experiment (no subprocess)._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_runner_simulation(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CampaignRunner orchestration with a stubbed _run_experiment (no subprocess)."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp1",
                description="Experiment 1",
                repetition_count=2,
            ),
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp2",
                description="Experiment 2",
                repetition_count=1,
            ),
        ],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "campaign_output"),
        comparison_pairs=[("exp1", "exp2")],
    )

    original_run = CampaignRunner._run_experiment

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir, multi_turn=None):
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.8},
        )

    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run

    try:
        runner = CampaignRunner(campaign, git_sha="test_sha")
        result = runner.run_campaign()
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original_run

    assert result.campaign_id == campaign.campaign_id
    assert len(result.runs) == 3  # 2 runs for exp1 + 1 run for exp2
    assert result.summary["total_runs"] == 3
    assert result.summary["successful_runs"] == 3
    assert "exp1_vs_exp2" in result.comparisons
```

**Inputs** (fixtures / parameters)

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_campaign_runner_with_failed_experiment`  (0.0048s)

_Test CampaignRunner handles failed experiments._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_runner_with_failed_experiment(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CampaignRunner handles failed experiments."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp_success",
                description="Successful experiment",
                repetition_count=1,
            ),
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp_fail",
                description="Failing experiment",
                repetition_count=1,
            ),
        ],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "campaign_output"),
    )

    # Override _run_experiment (the per-experiment unit) so no subprocess runs
    original_run = CampaignRunner._run_experiment

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir, multi_turn=None):
        success = experiment.name != "exp_fail"
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=success,
            error=None if success else "Simulated failure",
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.9} if success else {},
        )

    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run

    try:
        runner = CampaignRunner(campaign)
        result = runner.run_campaign()

        assert result.summary["total_runs"] == 2
        assert result.summary["successful_runs"] == 1
        assert result.summary["failed_runs"] == 1
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original_run
```

```python
def test_checkpoint_manager(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CheckpointManager save and load."""
    result = CampaignResult(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        campaign_hash="abc123",
        runs=[
            ExperimentRun(
                experiment_name="exp1",
                run_number=1,
                output_dir="experiments/exp1/run_1",
                success=True,
            ),
        ],
    )

    manager = CheckpointManager(tmp_path)
    manager.save_checkpoint(result)

    loaded = manager.load_checkpoint()

    assert loaded is not None
    assert loaded.campaign_id == result.campaign_id
    assert len(loaded.runs) == 1
```

```python
def test_checkpoint_manager_clear(tmp_path: Path):

        campaign_hash="abc123",
```

_Test experiment comparison logic._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_experiment_comparison():
    """Test experiment comparison logic."""
    runs_a = [
        ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name="exp_a",
            run_number=1,
            output_dir="exp_a/run_1",
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.80, "latency": 1.0},
        ),
        ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name="exp_a",
            run_number=2,
            output_dir="exp_a/run_2",
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.85, "latency": 1.1},
        ),
    ]

    runs_b = [
        ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name="exp_b",
            run_number=1,
            output_dir="exp_b/run_1",
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.90, "latency": 0.9},
        ),
    ]

    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="test"))
    comparison = runner._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b)

    assert comparison["status"] == "success"
    assert comparison["experiment_a"] == "exp_a"
    assert comparison["experiment_b"] == "exp_b"
    assert comparison["runs_a"] == 2
    assert comparison["runs_b"] == 1

    # Check task_success_rate delta
    tsr_delta = comparison["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]
    assert tsr_delta["a"] == pytest.approx(0.825)  # Average of 0.80 and 0.85
    assert tsr_delta["b"] == 0.90
    assert tsr_delta["delta"] == pytest.approx(0.075)
```

```python
def test_campaign_example_yaml_loads():
    """Test that the example campaign YAML can be loaded."""
    example_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "campaign" / "example_campaign.yaml"

    if example_path.exists():
        campaign = CampaignConfig.from_yaml(example_path)

        assert campaign.campaign_id == "campaign_001_baseline_vs_improved"
        assert len(campaign.experiments) == 4
        assert len(campaign.comparison_pairs) == 5
        assert campaign.statistical_settings["confidence_level"] == 0.95
```

```python
    exp = ExperimentConfig(
        name="exp_a",

        evaluation_config={"per_segment": 5},
```

```python
def test_build_config_overlay_uses_campaign_defaults():

        campaign_id="c",
        default_agents=["planner"],
        allow_missing_corpus_sources=False,

    ov = runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e"), 1)

    assert ae["allow_missing_corpus_sources"] is False
```

```python
def test_build_config_overlay_rejects_unmapped_keys():
    """Unknown agent_config/evaluation_config keys raise instead of being dropped."""
    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="c"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unmapped config keys"):
        runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e", agent_config={"bogus": 1}), 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unmapped config keys"):
        runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e", evaluation_config={"bogus": 1}), 1)
```

```python
def test_load_experiment_metrics_from_stdout(tmp_path: Path):
    """_load_experiment_metrics parses 'saved -> <dir>' and flattens metrics.json."""
    exp_dir = tmp_path / "exp_042"
    exp_dir.mkdir()
    (exp_dir / "metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "k": 10,
        "summary": {"planner": {"similar": {"all": {"task_success": 0.9, "n": 20}}}},
    }), encoding="utf-8")

    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="c"))
    metrics = runner._load_experiment_metrics(f"some output\nsaved -> {exp_dir}\n")
    assert metrics["planner_similar_task_success"] == 0.9

    # No 'saved ->' line or missing file -> empty dict, not an exception
    assert runner._load_experiment_metrics("no save line") == {}
    assert runner._load_experiment_metrics(f"saved -> {tmp_path / 'nope'}") == {}
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_auto_comparison_pairs_are_actually_used`  (0.0038s)

_parameter_grid + empty comparison_pairs -> auto-generated pairs must reach _run_comparisons._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_auto_comparison_pairs_are_actually_used(tmp_path: Path):
    """parameter_grid + empty comparison_pairs -> auto-generated pairs must reach _run_comparisons."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="grid_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        parameter_grid={"inference_mode": ["template", "llm"]},
        comparison_pairs=[],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "out"),
    )

    original_run = CampaignRunner._run_experiment

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir, multi_turn=None):
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.8},
        )

    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run
    try:
        result = CampaignRunner(campaign).run_campaign()
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original_run

    assert result.comparisons, "auto-generated comparison pairs were dropped"
    (pair_key,) = result.comparisons.keys()
    assert pair_key.endswith("_vs_" + pair_key.split("_vs_")[-1])
    assert result.comparisons[pair_key]["status"] == "success"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_statistical_settings_produce_ci_and_paired_delta`  (0.0109s)

_statistical_settings from campaign.yaml drives bootstrap CIs (Phase 4.1)._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_statistical_settings_produce_ci_and_paired_delta():
    """statistical_settings from campaign.yaml drives bootstrap CIs (Phase 4.1)."""
    cfg = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="stat_test",
        statistical_settings={
            "confidence_level": 0.95,
            "bootstrap_samples": 200,
            "paired_comparison": True,
            "random_seed": 7,
        },
    )
    runner = CampaignRunner(cfg)
    runs_a, runs_b = _stat_runs()
    comparison = runner._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b)

    assert comparison["statistics"]["confidence_level"] == 0.95
    assert comparison["statistics"]["bootstrap_samples"] == 200

    tsr = comparison["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]
    # Paired equal-length runs → delta CI + p_value present
    assert "delta_ci" in tsr and "p_value" in tsr and "significant" in tsr
    # B is consistently ~0.10 above A → CI excludes 0 → significant
    assert tsr["significant"] is True
    assert tsr["delta_ci"][0] > 0

    # Per-experiment mean/std/CI across runs
    stats = comparison["metric_stats"]["task_success_rate"]
    assert stats["a"]["n"] == 3 and stats["b"]["n"] == 3
    assert stats["a"]["ci"][0] <= stats["a"]["mean"] <= stats["a"]["ci"][1]
```

_paired_comparison=false or unequal run counts → no delta_ci._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_unpaired_or_disabled_statistics_skip_delta_ci():
    """paired_comparison=false or unequal run counts → no delta_ci."""
    cfg = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="stat_test2",
        statistical_settings={"paired_comparison": False, "bootstrap_samples": 100},
    )
    runner = CampaignRunner(cfg)
    runs_a, runs_b = _stat_runs()
    comparison = runner._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b)
    assert "delta_ci" not in comparison["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]

    # Unequal lengths with paired enabled → also no delta_ci
    cfg2 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="stat_test3",
        statistical_settings={"paired_comparison": True, "bootstrap_samples": 100},
    )
    runner2 = CampaignRunner(cfg2)
    comparison2 = runner2._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b[:2])
    assert "delta_ci" not in comparison2["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]
```

```python
def test_repo_campaign_yaml_still_loads_via_config_system():
    """Regression: configs/evaluation/campaign.yaml parses identically through load_config."""
    from package.config import CONFIG_DIR

    campaign = CampaignConfig.from_yaml(CONFIG_DIR / "evaluation" / "campaign.yaml")

    assert campaign.campaign_id == "campaign_001_parameter_grid"
    assert campaign.statistical_settings["confidence_level"] == 0.95
    assert len(campaign.expand_parameter_grid()) == 12  # 3 agents x 2 modes x 2 reflect_rounds
```

_configs/-style campaign files are schema-validated with extra=forbid._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_config_rejects_unknown_keys(tmp_path):
    """configs/-style campaign files are schema-validated with extra=forbid."""
    from package.config.load_config import ConfigError, load_config

    cfg_dir = tmp_path / "evaluation"
    cfg_dir.mkdir()
    (cfg_dir / "campaign.yaml").write_text('campaign_id: "x"\nbogus_key: 1\n', encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
```

```python
def test_campaign_config_overlay_is_honored(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """CampaignConfig.from_yaml routes configs/ paths through load_config (CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH applies)."""
    from package.config import CONFIG_DIR

    overlay = tmp_path / "overlay.yaml"
    overlay.write_text(
        'evaluation/campaign:\n  output_dir: "agent_eval/campaigns/overlaid_test"\n',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay))

    campaign = CampaignConfig.from_yaml(CONFIG_DIR / "evaluation" / "campaign.yaml")

    assert campaign.output_dir == "agent_eval/campaigns/overlaid_test"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_run_experiment_builds_domain_cmd_and_runs_subprocess`  (0.0245s)

__run_experiment (non-dry-run) must reach subprocess.run: cmd carries --domain_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_run_experiment_builds_domain_cmd_and_runs_subprocess(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """_run_experiment (non-dry-run) must reach subprocess.run: cmd carries --domain
    (no --tag) and traj-path resolution works — regression for the UnboundLocalError
    caused by a stray function-local 'from package.config import get_data_paths'."""
    import subprocess as sp

    exp_dir = tmp_path / "exp_out"
    exp_dir.mkdir()
    (exp_dir / "metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps({"summary": {}}), encoding="utf-8")

    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="c",
        dataset={"split": "validation", "filters": {"domains": ["Video_Games", "Electronics", "Toys_and_Games"]}},
        experiments=[ExperimentConfig(name="exp1")],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "campaign_output"),
    )
    runner = CampaignRunner(campaign, git_sha="test_sha")

    captured = {}

    class _Result:
        returncode = 0
        stdout = f"noise\nsaved -> {exp_dir}\n"
        stderr = ""

    def fake_run(cmd, **kwargs):
        captured["cmd"] = cmd
        return _Result()

    monkeypatch.setattr(sp, "run", fake_run)

    run = runner._run_experiment(campaign.experiments[0], 1, tmp_path / "out")

    assert run.success is True
    assert run.error is None
    cmd = captured["cmd"]
    assert "--tag" not in cmd
    assert cmd.count("--domain") == 3
    assert "scripts/evaluation/run_agent_eval.py" in " ".join(cmd)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_real_campaign_overlay_loads_via_config_system`  (0.0222s)

_Regression: a real CampaignRunner overlay must pass _check_overlay_keys._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_campaign_overlay_loads_via_config_system(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Regression: a real CampaignRunner overlay must pass _check_overlay_keys.

    campaign_id was set by the overlay but absent from agent_eval.yaml, so every
    campaign experiment died at startup (ConfigError: unknown key). This drives
    the actual overlay through CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH + load_config.
    """
    import yaml
    from package.config.load_config import load_config

    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="mt_campaign"))
    exp = ExperimentConfig(
        name="exp_a",
        agent_config={"agents": ["baseline"], "inference_mode": "template"},
        evaluation_config={"per_segment": 5},
        random_seed=7,
    )

    for run_number in (1, 2):
        overlay = runner._build_config_overlay(exp, run_number)
        overlay_path = tmp_path / f"overlay_{run_number}.yaml"
        overlay_path.write_text(
            yaml.safe_dump(overlay, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay_path))

        cfg = load_config("agent/agent_eval")["agent_eval"]
        assert cfg["campaign_id"] == "mt_campaign"
        assert cfg["agents"] == ["baseline"]
        assert cfg["per_segment"] == 5
        assert cfg["trajectory_file"] == f"mt_campaign_exp_a_run{run_number}_trajectories.jsonl"

        inference = load_config("agent/inference")["inference"]
        assert inference["mode"] == "template"
        baselines = load_config("eval/baselines")["baselines"]
        assert baselines["seed"] == 7
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_build_config_overlay_pins_experiment_dir`  (0.0272s)

_campaign_dir pins exp artifacts into <campaign>/<exp>/run_N via experiment_dir._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_build_config_overlay_pins_experiment_dir(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """campaign_dir pins exp artifacts into <campaign>/<exp>/run_N via experiment_dir."""
    import os
    import yaml
    from package.config import get_data_paths
    from package.config.load_config import load_config

    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="c"))
    campaign_dir = tmp_path / "campaigns" / "c1"
    exp = ExperimentConfig(name="exp_a")

    ov = runner._build_config_overlay(exp, 2, campaign_dir=campaign_dir)
    run_dir = campaign_dir / "exp_a" / "run_2"
    assert ov["agent/agent_eval"]["agent_eval"]["experiment_dir"] == str(run_dir)
    # No exp_NNN relocation anymore — the run dir IS the experiment dir.
    assert "outputs" not in ov.get("path/data_paths", {})

    # The fragment must survive the real overlay path.
    overlay_path = tmp_path / "config_overlay.yaml"
    overlay_path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(ov, sort_keys=False), encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay_path))
    ae = load_config("agent/agent_eval")["agent_eval"]
    assert ae["experiment_dir"] == str(run_dir)
    assert ae["item_knn_reuse_cache"] is True

    # ItemKNN cache is campaign-scoped and resolves under the campaign dir.
    knn_path = get_data_paths().item_knn_path("validation")
    assert Path(os.path.normpath(knn_path)).is_relative_to(
        Path(os.path.normpath(campaign_dir / "item_knn"))
    )
    _ = load_config("path/data_paths")  # must not raise on the extra fragment

    # Without campaign_dir nothing is pinned — shared pool + defaults untouched.
    ov_default = runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e"), 1)
    ae_default = ov_default["agent/agent_eval"]["agent_eval"]
    assert "experiment_dir" not in ae_default
    assert "item_knn_reuse_cache" not in ae_default
    assert "path/data_paths" not in ov_default
```

```python
def test_experiment_run_roundtrip_includes_experiment_dir():
    run = ExperimentRun(
        experiment_name="e",
        run_number=1,
        output_dir="/x",
        experiment_dir="/x/experiments/exp_001",
        success=True,
    )
    assert ExperimentRun.from_dict(run.to_dict()).experiment_dir == "/x/experiments/exp_001"
    # Old campaign_result.json files without the key still load.
    old = run.to_dict()
    del old["experiment_dir"]
    assert ExperimentRun.from_dict(old).experiment_dir == ""
```

```python
def test_dict_rejects_output_dir_and_unknown_keys(self):
    n = CampaignRunner._normalize_multi_turn_param
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="output_dir"):
        n({"output_dir": "/tmp/x"})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unmapped keys"):
        n({"bogus": 1})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="bool or dict"):
        n("yes")
```

_No agent_config.multi_turn anywhere -> experiment[0] run 1 still runs it._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_once_without_optin_keeps_legacy_first_run(self, tmp_path):
    """No agent_config.multi_turn anywhere -> experiment[0] run 1 still runs it."""
    runner = CampaignRunner(_campaign(tmp_path))
    experiments = runner.config.expand_parameter_grid()
    plans = runner._multi_turn_plans(experiments, tmp_path / "out")
    assert set(plans) == {(0, 1)}
    frag = plans[(0, 1)]
    assert frag["enabled"] is True
    assert frag["output_dir"].endswith(str(Path("multi_turn") / "exp_first_run1"))
```

```python
def test_once_picks_first_optin_run_only(self, tmp_path):
    runner = CampaignRunner(
        _campaign(tmp_path, agent_multi_turn=True, repetitions=2)
    )
    experiments = runner.config.expand_parameter_grid()
    plans = runner._multi_turn_plans(experiments, tmp_path / "out")
    # exp_first opts in but 'once' caps at its first repetition.
    assert set(plans) == {(0, 1)}
```

```python
    assert set(plans) == {(1, 1)}
```

```python
def test_param_dict_reaches_plan(self, tmp_path):
    runner = CampaignRunner(
        _campaign(tmp_path, multi_turn="all",
                  agent_multi_turn={"limits": {"max_turns": 4}, "timeout_seconds": 60})

    assert frag["timeout_seconds"] == 60
    assert frag["enabled"] is True
```

```python
def test_overlay_enables_and_redirects_output_dir(self, tmp_path):
```

```python
def test_success_status_runs_and_metrics(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    campaign = _campaign(tmp_path)
    out_dir = tmp_path / "campaign_output"
    _stub_runs(monkeypatch)

    result = CampaignRunner(campaign).run_campaign()

    mt = result.multi_turn
    assert mt["status"] == "success"
    assert mt["mode"] == "once"
    assert len(mt["runs"]) == 1
    entry = mt["runs"][0]
    assert entry["experiment"] == "exp_first" and entry["run_number"] == 1
    assert entry["status"] == "success"
    assert entry["metrics"]["turn_success_rate"] == 0.9
    assert "exp_first_run1" in entry["path"]
    on_disk = json.loads((out_dir / "campaign_result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert on_disk["multi_turn"]["status"] == "success"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_multi_turn_wiring.py::TestCampaignResultMultiTurn::test_not_run_when_run_succeeded_but_no_file`  (0.0143s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_not_run_when_run_succeeded_but_no_file(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    campaign = _campaign(tmp_path)
    # Enabled run (legacy fallback) but no artifact written: simulate by
    # making the stub skip file creation for this run.
    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir, multi_turn=None):
        run_dir = output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        frag = multi_turn if multi_turn is not None else {"enabled": False}
        (run_dir / "config_overlay.yaml").write_text(
            yaml.safe_dump({"agent/agent_eval": {"agent_eval": {"multi_turn": frag}}}),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name, run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(run_dir), success=True, metrics={},
        )
    monkeypatch.setattr(CampaignRunner, "_run_experiment", fake_run)

    result = CampaignRunner(campaign).run_campaign()

    entry = result.multi_turn["runs"][0]
    assert entry["status"] == "not_run"
    assert "produced no multi_turn.json" in entry["reason"]
```

```python
def test_no_optin_and_never_run_has_empty_runs(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Stub that writes no overlay at all -> collector finds nothing enabled."""
    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir, multi_turn=None):
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name, run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=True, metrics={},
        )
    monkeypatch.setattr(CampaignRunner, "_run_experiment", fake_run)

    result = CampaignRunner(_campaign(tmp_path)).run_campaign()

    assert result.multi_turn["status"] == "not_run"
    assert result.multi_turn["runs"] == []
```

```python
def test_result_roundtrip_preserves_multi_turn(self, tmp_path):
    result = CampaignResult(
        campaign_id="c", campaign_hash="h",
        multi_turn={"status": "success", "mode": "all",
                    "runs": [{"experiment": "e", "run_number": 1,
                              "status": "success", "path": "/x/multi_turn.json",
                              "metrics": {"turn_success_rate": 0.5}}]},
    )
    restored = CampaignResult.from_dict(result.to_dict())
    assert restored.multi_turn == result.multi_turn
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_multi_turn_wiring.py::TestOverlayPassesConfigCheck::test_real_campaign_overlay_loads_via_config_system`  (0.0264s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_campaign_overlay_loads_via_config_system(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    runner = CampaignRunner(_campaign(tmp_path))
    experiment = ExperimentConfig(
        name="exp_x",
        agent_config={"agents": ["baseline"], "multi_turn": {"timeout_seconds": 60}},
        evaluation_config={"per_segment": 3},
        repetition_count=1,
    )
    output_dir = tmp_path / "campaign_output"
    plans = runner._multi_turn_plans([experiment], output_dir)
    # Both an enabled run (param dict opt-in under "all") and a disabled
    # run must survive the fail-closed overlay key check.
    for frag in (plans[(0, 1)], None):
        overlay = runner._build_config_overlay(experiment, 1, multi_turn=frag)
        overlay_path = tmp_path / f"overlay_{frag is not None}.yaml"
        overlay_path.write_text(
            yaml.safe_dump(overlay, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay_path))
        cfg = load_config("agent/agent_eval")["agent_eval"]
        assert cfg["campaign_id"] == "mt_campaign"
        assert cfg["agents"] == ["baseline"]
        assert cfg["multi_turn"]["enabled"] is (frag is not None)
        if frag is not None:
            assert cfg["multi_turn"]["timeout_seconds"] == 60
            assert cfg["multi_turn"]["output_dir"].endswith("exp_x_run1")
```

```python
def test_items_satisfying_needs_known_price_and_category(corpus):
    ids = [VG_A, VG_B, VG_C, EL_A]
    assert items_satisfying(corpus, ids, 20, "Accessories") == {VG_C}  # VG_B has no price, EL_A costs 25.99
    assert items_satisfying(corpus, ids, None, "Accessories") == {VG_C, EL_A}
    assert items_satisfying(corpus, ids, 20, None) == {VG_A, VG_C}
    assert items_satisfying(corpus, [], 20, "Accessories") == set()
    assert items_without_price(corpus, [VG_A, VG_B]) == {VG_B}
```

```python
def test_build_requests_makes_every_kind_with_the_right_ground_truth(corpus):
    reqs = _requests(corpus)
    assert sorted(reqs) == ["ambiguous-none-0", "ambiguous-none-1", "cold_text-cold-0", "constrained-warm-0",
                            "exclusion-warm-0", "personalized-warm-0", "similar-warm-0", "unanswerable-none-0"]
    similar = reqs["similar-warm-0"]
    assert similar.seed_item_id == VG_A and VG_A in similar.text and similar.targets == [1, 2] and not similar.oracle
    constrained = reqs["constrained-warm-0"]  # VG_B has no price, so VG_C (5.5, "Accessories") seeds the constraint
    assert constrained.category == "Accessories" and constrained.price_max == math.ceil(5.5 * 1.25 / 5) * 5
    assert constrained.targets == [2] and "Accessories" in constrained.text and "10" in constrained.text and constrained.oracle
    cold = reqs["cold_text-cold-0"]
    assert cold.category == "Electronics" and cold.targets == [3, 4] and cold.oracle
    assert reqs["ambiguous-none-0"].user_id is None and reqs["ambiguous-none-0"].targets == []

    # Check exclusion request
    exclusion = reqs["exclusion-warm-0"]
    assert exclusion.user_id is not None
    assert exclusion.targets  # should have targets (held-out items except the excluded one)

    # Check unanswerable request
    unanswerable = reqs["unanswerable-none-0"]
    assert unanswerable.user_id is None
    assert unanswerable.targets == []
```

```python
def test_request_scope_keeps_target_informed_cases_separate(corpus):
    requests = list(_requests(corpus).values())
    realistic = filter_requests_by_scope(requests, "realistic")
    oracle = filter_requests_by_scope(requests, "oracle")
    assert realistic and all(not request.oracle for request in realistic)
    assert oracle and all(request.oracle for request in oracle)
    assert {request.request_id for request in realistic}.isdisjoint({request.request_id for request in oracle})
    assert len(filter_requests_by_scope(requests, "all")) == len(requests)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="scope"):
        filter_requests_by_scope(requests, "unknown")
```

```python
def test_extract_recommendations_prefers_ranking_then_retrieval_then_query():
    ranked = {"tool": "RankTool", "ok": True, "data": {"ranked": [{"item_id": "a"}, {"item_id": "b"}, {"item_id": "c"}]}}
    cands = {"tool": "SearchTool", "ok": True, "data": {"candidates": [{"item_id": "x"}, {"item_id": "y"}]}}
    fetched = {"tool": "LookupTool", "ok": True, "data": {"items": [{"item_id": "z"}]}}
    assert extract_recommendations([cands, ranked, fetched], 2) == ["a", "b"]
    assert extract_recommendations([cands, fetched], 5) == ["x", "y"]
    assert extract_recommendations([fetched], 5) == ["z"]
    assert extract_recommendations([{**ranked, "ok": False}], 5) == []
    assert extract_recommendations([], 5) == []
```

```python
def test_score_trajectory_ranking_and_tool_selection(corpus):
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="similar-warm-0", kind="similar", segment="warm", text="t", targets=[1])
    traj = _trajectory(steps=[_step("SimilarTool"), _step("RankTool")], recommended=[VG_A, VG_B])
    row = score_trajectory(request, traj, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
    assert row["recall@2"] == 1.0 and row["hit_rate@2"] == 1.0 and row["mrr@2"] == 0.5
    assert row["ndcg@2"] == pytest.approx(1 / math.log2(3))
    assert row["precision@2"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert row["map@2"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert row["tool_selection_ok"] is True and row["task_success"] is True and row["n_steps"] == 2
    wrong_order = _trajectory(steps=[_step("RankTool"), _step("SimilarTool")], recommended=[VG_A])
    assert score_trajectory(request, wrong_order, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)["tool_selection_ok"] is False
```

```python
def test_summarize_means_per_agent_kind_and_segment():
    rows = [
        {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "a", "kind": "similar", "segment": "warm", "task_success": True, "recall@10": 0.5, "x": None},
        {"request_id": "r1", "agent": "a", "kind": "similar", "segment": "warm", "task_success": False, "recall@10": 0.0, "x": None},
        {"request_id": "r2", "agent": "a", "kind": "similar", "segment": "sparse", "task_success": True, "recall@10": 1.0, "x": None},
    ]
    out = summarize(rows)["a"]["similar"]
    assert out["warm"]["n"] == 2 and out["warm"]["task_success"] == 0.5 and out["warm"]["recall@10"] == 0.25
    assert out["all"]["n"] == 3 and out["all"]["recall@10"] == pytest.approx(0.5) and "x" not in out["all"]
```

```python
def test_memory_tool_refuses_interactions_from_the_future(corpus, cf_setup):
    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    interactions = interactions.assign(timestamp=np.arange(len(interactions)) + 10)
    with pytest.raises(LeakageError):
        MemoryTool(interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, as_of_timestamp=5)
    MemoryTool(interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, as_of_timestamp=int(interactions["timestamp"].max()))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        MemoryTool(interactions.drop(columns="timestamp"), user2id, item2id, corpus, as_of_timestamp=5)
```

_MemoryTool should return recent_items field with most recent items first._

```python
def test_memory_tool_returns_recent_items(corpus, cf_setup):
    """MemoryTool should return recent_items field with most recent items first."""
    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    memory = MemoryTool(interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus)

    # Get a user with interactions
    user_id = "u1"
    result = memory.execute(user_id)

    assert "recent_items" in result
    assert isinstance(result["recent_items"], list)
    # If user has interactions, recent_items should not be empty
    if result["known"]:
        assert len(result["recent_items"]) > 0
        # All items should be namespaced
        for item in result["recent_items"]:
            assert "::" in item
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_agent_eval.py::test_memory_tool_recent_items_respects_as_of_timestamp`  (0.0167s)

_recent_items should not include interactions after as_of_timestamp._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_memory_tool_recent_items_respects_as_of_timestamp(corpus, cf_setup):
    """recent_items should not include interactions after as_of_timestamp."""
    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    interactions = interactions.assign(timestamp=np.arange(len(interactions)))

    # as_of_timestamp in the middle of interactions
    as_of = len(interactions) // 2
    # Filter interactions to only include those <= as_of before passing to MemoryTool
    # (caller's responsibility to provide valid data for the snapshot)
    valid_interactions = interactions[interactions["timestamp"] <= as_of].copy()
    memory = MemoryTool(valid_interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, as_of_timestamp=as_of)

    user_id = "u1"
    result = memory.execute(user_id)

    if result["known"]:
        # recent_items should only include items with timestamp <= as_of
        assert "recent_items" in result
        assert isinstance(result["recent_items"], list)
```

```python
def test_memory_tool_recent_items_count_limited(corpus, cf_setup):

    user_id = "u1"
```

```python
def test_personalized_is_not_scored_for_a_planner_that_cannot_read_history(corpus):

    blind = score_trajectory(request, _trajectory(ask_user="What do you like?"), item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
```

```python
def test_score_trajectory_still_scores_old_trajectories_without_the_new_fields(corpus):
    traj = _trajectory(steps=[_step("SimilarTool"), _step("RankTool")], recommended=[VG_A, VG_B])
```

```python
def test_score_trajectory_still_scores_old_trajectories_without_args_and_output_summary(corpus):
    """Old trajectories without args/output_summary fields should still be scored."""
    # Create a step without the new fields (as it would be in old trajectories)
    old_step = {"tool": "SimilarTool", "ok": True, "error": None, "invented_ids": [], "latency_seconds": 0.01}
    traj = _trajectory(steps=[old_step, _step("RankTool")], recommended=[VG_A, VG_B])
    # Manually ensure the first step doesn't have the new fields
    traj["steps"][0] = old_step
    assert "args" not in traj["steps"][0]
    assert "output_summary" not in traj["steps"][0]
    row = score_trajectory(_similar_request(), traj, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
    assert row["task_success"] is True
```

```python
def test_summarize_averages_first_attempt_ok_and_skips_missing():

        {"request_id": f"r{i}", "agent": "planner", "kind": "similar", "segment": "warm", "first_attempt_ok": v, "reflections": r}
        for i, (v, r) in enumerate([(True, 0), (False, 1), (False, 1), (None, 0)])

    stats = summarize(rows)["planner"]["similar"]["all"]
```

```python
def test_run_planner_records_reflections_and_the_last_attempt(corpus):
    import json

    from package.agents import PlanExecutor
    from package.tools import FilterTool
    from tests.agents.test_plan_executor import ScriptedLLM

    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    bad = {"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"category": "Games"}}
    good = {"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"domain": "Video_Games"}, "limit": 2}}
    llm = ScriptedLLM(json.dumps({"steps": [bad]}), json.dumps({"steps": [good]}))
    executor = PlanExecutor(llm, [FilterTool(corpus)], reflect_rounds=1)
    traj = script.run_planner(executor, _similar_request(), "planner", 2)
    assert traj["reflections"] == 1 and traj["first_attempt_ok"] is False and traj["reflect_rounds"] == 1
    assert [s["ok"] for s in traj["steps"]] == [True] and traj["plan"] == [good] and traj["recommended"]
    assert traj["usage"]["attempts"] == 2 and traj["usage"]["prompt_tokens"] == 20
    json.dumps(traj)  # the file stays serialisable
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_agent_eval.py::test_trajectory_error_chars_comes_from_yaml_and_must_be_positive`  (0.0263s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_trajectory_error_chars_comes_from_yaml_and_must_be_positive(corpus, monkeypatch):
    from package.agents import PlanExecutor
    from package.config import load_config
    from package.config.load_config import ConfigError
    from package.tools import FilterTool
    from tests.agents.test_plan_executor import ScriptedLLM

    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    assert script._error_chars() == load_config("agent/agent_eval")["agent_eval"]["trajectory_error_chars"]

    def with_chars(value):
        def fake(name, *args, **kwargs):
            cfg = load_config(name, *args, **kwargs)
            if name == "agent/agent_eval":
                cfg["agent_eval"]["trajectory_error_chars"] = value
            return cfg
        return fake

    monkeypatch.setattr(script, "load_config", with_chars(12))
    executor = PlanExecutor(ScriptedLLM("nope"), [FilterTool(corpus)], plan_retries=0, reflect_rounds=0)
    traj = script.run_planner(executor, _similar_request(), "planner", 2)
    assert 0 < len(traj["error"]) <= 12
    monkeypatch.setattr(script, "load_config", with_chars(0))
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        script._error_chars()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_agent_eval.py::test_resume_drops_planner_records_made_with_another_reflect_setting`  (0.0039s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_drops_planner_records_made_with_another_reflect_setting():
    keep = _load_run_agent_eval().is_resumable
    text_of = {"r0": "hello"}
    rec = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "planner", "text": "hello", "reflect_rounds": 1}
    assert keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 1)
    assert not keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0)  # a 0-vs-1 comparison must not mix runs
    old = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != "reflect_rounds"}  # written before plan-reflect existed = 0 rounds
    assert keep(old, text_of, ["planner"], 0) and not keep(old, text_of, ["planner"], 1)
    assert not keep({**rec, "text": "changed"}, text_of, ["planner"], 1) and not keep(rec, text_of, ["baseline"], 1)
    assert keep({"request_id": "r0", "agent": "baseline", "text": "hello"}, text_of, ["baseline"], 1)  # no LLM, no setting
```

_campaign_id is provenance, not protocol: it must not change the resume hash._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_agent_config_excludes_campaign_id():
    """campaign_id is provenance, not protocol: it must not change the resume hash."""
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    base = {"agents": ["baseline"], "per_segment": 3, "trajectory_file": "t.jsonl"}
    variants = [
        dict(base),
        {**base, "campaign_id": None},
        {**base, "campaign_id": "campaign_a"},
        {**base, "campaign_id": "campaign_b"},
    ]
    normalized = [script._resume_agent_config(v) for v in variants]
    assert all(n == normalized[0] for n in normalized)
    assert "campaign_id" not in normalized[0]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_agent_eval.py::test_resume_agent_config_excludes_multi_turn`  (0.0038s)

_Toggling agent_eval.multi_turn must not change the resume hash input._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_agent_config_excludes_multi_turn():
    """Toggling agent_eval.multi_turn must not change the resume hash input."""
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    base = {"agents": ["baseline"], "k": 10, "per_segment": 20}
    variants = [
        dict(base),
        dict(base, multi_turn={"enabled": False, "dataset": "d.jsonl", "limits": {}}),
        dict(base, multi_turn={"enabled": True, "dataset": "d.jsonl",
                               "output_dir": "/tmp/x", "limits": {"max_turns": 3},
                               "timeout_seconds": 60}),
        dict(base, campaign_id="campaign_a"),
        dict(base, campaign_id="campaign_b",
             multi_turn={"enabled": True, "limits": {"max_turns": 9}}),
    ]
    hashed = [script._resume_agent_config(cfg) for cfg in variants]
    assert hashed[0] == hashed[1] == hashed[2] == hashed[3] == hashed[4]
    assert all("multi_turn" not in h and "campaign_id" not in h for h in hashed)
```

_A crashing multi-turn step must not sink the already-paid agent eval:_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_run_optional_multi_turn_swallows_step_errors(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """A crashing multi-turn step must not sink the already-paid agent eval:
    it returns status=error so metrics.json still gets written."""
    import package.evaluation.multi_turn as mt_pkg

    def _boom(*a, **k):
        raise RuntimeError("simulated multi-turn crash")

    monkeypatch.setattr(mt_pkg, "run_multi_turn_for_eval", _boom)
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    info = script.run_optional_multi_turn(
        {"multi_turn": {"enabled": True}}, tmp_path / "exp_001"
    )
    assert info["status"] == "error"
    assert "simulated multi-turn crash" in info["error"]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_behavior_snapshot.py::test_behavior_snapshot_and_save`  (0.1193s)

_Generate and save behavior snapshot for later comparison._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_behavior_snapshot_and_save(tmp_path, corpus):
    """Generate and save behavior snapshot for later comparison."""
    trajectories = _make_comprehensive_trajectories()

    # Score all trajectories
    rows = []
    for request, traj in trajectories:
        row = score_trajectory(request, traj, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
        rows.append(row)

    # Summarize
    summary = summarize(rows)

    # Convert to JSON-serializable format
    snapshot = {
        "rows": rows,
        "summary": summary,
    }

    # Save snapshot
    snapshot_path = tmp_path / "behavior_snapshot.json"
    with open(snapshot_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(snapshot, f, indent=2, default=str)

    # Also save to the expected location if this is the first run
    if not SNAPSHOT_FILE.exists():
        SNAPSHOT_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(SNAPSHOT_FILE, "w") as f:
            json.dump(snapshot, f, indent=2, default=str)

    # Verify snapshot has data
    assert len(rows) > 0
    assert len(summary) > 0

    # Check key columns exist
    sample_row = rows[0]
    expected_id_keys = {"request_id", "agent", "kind", "segment"}
    assert expected_id_keys.issubset(sample_row.keys())

    # Check ranking columns for non-ambiguous requests with targets
    for row in rows:
        if row["kind"] != "ambiguous" and row.get("request_id", "").startswith("baseline-similar-0"):
            assert f"precision@2" in row
            assert f"recall@2" in row
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_behavior_snapshot.py::test_behavior_snapshot_matches_existing`  (0.1189s)

_Verify current behavior matches the saved snapshot._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_behavior_snapshot_matches_existing(corpus):
    """Verify current behavior matches the saved snapshot."""
    if not SNAPSHOT_FILE.exists():
        pytest.skip(f"Snapshot file {SNAPSHOT_FILE} does not exist. Run test_behavior_snapshot_and_save first.")

    with open(SNAPSHOT_FILE, "r") as f:
        expected = json.load(f)

    trajectories = _make_comprehensive_trajectories()

    # Score all trajectories
    rows = []
    for request, traj in trajectories:
        row = score_trajectory(request, traj, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
        rows.append(row)

    # Summarize
    summary = summarize(rows)

    # Compare rows
    assert len(rows) == len(expected["rows"]), f"Row count mismatch: {len(rows)} vs {len(expected['rows'])}"

    for i, (actual_row, expected_row) in enumerate(zip(rows, expected["rows"])):
        assert actual_row.keys() == expected_row.keys(), f"Row {i} keys mismatch"
        for key in actual_row.keys():
            actual_val = actual_row[key]
            expected_val = expected_row[key]
            # Handle NaN and floating point comparison
            if isinstance(actual_val, float) and isinstance(expected_val, float):
                if math.isnan(actual_val) and math.isnan(expected_val):
                    continue
                assert actual_val == pytest.approx(expected_val, rel=1e-6), f"Row {i}, key {key}: {actual_val} vs {expected_val}"
            else:
                assert actual_val == expected_val, f"Row {i}, key {key}: {actual_val} vs {expected_val}"

    # Compare summary
    assert summary.keys() == expected["summary"].keys()
    for agent in summary:
        assert summary[agent].keys() == expected["summary"][agent].keys()
        for kind in summary[agent]:
            assert summary[agent][kind].keys() == expected["summary"][agent][kind].keys()
            for segment in summary[agent][kind]:
                actual_seg = summary[agent][kind][segment]
                expected_seg = expected["summary"][agent][kind][segment]
                assert actual_seg.keys() == expected_seg.keys()
                for key in actual_seg:
                    actual_val = actual_seg[key]
                    expected_val = expected_seg[key]
                    if isinstance(actual_val, float) and isinstance(expected_val, float):
                        if math.isnan(actual_val) and math.isnan(expected_val):
                            continue
                        assert actual_val == pytest.approx(expected_val, rel=1e-6), f"Summary {agent}/{kind}/{segment}/{key}: {actual_val} vs {expected_val}"
                    else:
                        assert actual_val == expected_val, f"Summary {agent}/{kind}/{segment}/{key}: {actual_val} vs {expected_val}"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_config_resume_integration.py::test_resume_with_modified_config_clears_cache`  (0.0213s)

_Test that modifying a config file between runs clears the LRU cache._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_with_modified_config_clears_cache(tmp_path):
    """
    Test that modifying a config file between runs clears the LRU cache.
    This simulates the scenario where a user edits configs/agent/agent.yaml
    and then re-runs with --resume.
    """
    # Clear cache to avoid interference from other tests
    _file_mtime_cache.clear()

    agent_dir = tmp_path / "configs" / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Create initial config
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # First load
    cfg1 = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg1["agent"]["max_steps"] == 8
    assert cfg1["agent"]["type1"]["candidate_multiplier"] == 5

    # Wait to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify config
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 16\n  plan_retries: 2\n  reflect_rounds: 2\n  reflect_error_chars: 500\n"
        "  max_tokens: 4096\n  temperature: 0.1\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 10\n    min_candidates: 100\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # Second load should get new values
    cfg2 = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg2["agent"]["max_steps"] == 16
    assert cfg2["agent"]["type1"]["candidate_multiplier"] == 10
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_invalid_config_rejected(tmp_path):
    """
    Test that if a config file is modified to an invalid state,
    loading it raises ConfigError.
    """
    # Clear cache to avoid interference from other tests
    _file_mtime_cache.clear()

    agent_dir = tmp_path / "configs" / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Create valid initial config
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # First load succeeds
    cfg1 = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg1["agent"]["max_steps"] == 8

    # Wait to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify to invalid config (temperature out of range)
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 3.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # Second load should fail validation
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="Config validation failed"):
        load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_config_resume_integration.py::test_multiple_config_files_cache_invalidation`  (0.0278s)

_Test that modifying multiple config files clears their respective caches._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_multiple_config_files_cache_invalidation(tmp_path):
    """
    Test that modifying multiple config files clears their respective caches.
    """
    # Clear cache to avoid interference from other tests
    _file_mtime_cache.clear()

    agent_dir = tmp_path / "configs" / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Create multiple configs
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    (agent_dir / "tools.yaml").write_text(
        "tools:\n  max_candidates: 1000\n  max_query_rows: 100\n  sql_time_budget_seconds: 5.0\n"
        "  max_output_summary_chars: 500\n  defaults:\n    sql_limit: 100\n    item_cf_top_k: 20\n"
        "    query_max_rows: 20\n    memory_top_n_categories: 3\n    memory_recent_items: 5\n"
        "  semantic_search:\n    default_limit: 50\n    max_query_tokens: 20\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # Load both configs
    cfg_agent1 = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    cfg_tools1 = load_config("agent/tools", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg_agent1["agent"]["max_steps"] == 8
    assert cfg_tools1["tools"]["max_candidates"] == 1000

    # Wait to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify only agent.yaml
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 16\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # agent.yaml should be reloaded, tools.yaml should stay cached
    cfg_agent2 = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    cfg_tools2 = load_config("agent/tools", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg_agent2["agent"]["max_steps"] == 16  # New value
    assert cfg_tools2["tools"]["max_candidates"] == 1000  # Cached value
```

```python
def test_config_with_env_var_interpolation_and_validation(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """
    Test that env var interpolation works together with schema validation.
    """
    # Clear cache to avoid interference from other tests
    _file_mtime_cache.clear()

    agent_dir = tmp_path / "configs" / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Config with env var
    monkeypatch.setenv("TEST_STEPS", "12")
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: ${TEST_STEPS}\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    cfg = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg["agent"]["max_steps"] == 12

    # Change env var to invalid value
    monkeypatch.setenv("TEST_STEPS", "-5")
    # Reload should fail validation
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="Config validation failed"):
        load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
```

```python
def test_new_columns_do_not_change_any_existing_column(corpus):
    bare = _trajectory(steps=[_step("SimilarTool"), _step("RankTool")], recommended=[VG_A, VG_B])
    row_bare = score_trajectory(_request(), bare, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
    row_full = score_trajectory(_request(), _answered(), item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
    assert {k: v for k, v in row_full.items() if k not in NEW_COLUMNS} == {k: v for k, v in row_bare.items() if k not in NEW_COLUMNS}
    assert row_full["task_success"] is True and row_full["recall@2"] == row_bare["recall@2"]
```

```python
def test_summarize_averages_the_answer_columns_and_skips_missing():
    base = {"agent": "planner", "kind": "similar", "segment": "warm"}
    rows = [
        {**base, "request_id": "a", "answer_grounded": True, "answer_fallback": False, "answer_status_ok": True, "answer_status_tool_error": False},
        {**base, "request_id": "b", "answer_grounded": True, "answer_fallback": True, "answer_status_ok": True, "answer_status_tool_error": False},
        {**base, "request_id": "c", "answer_grounded": None, "answer_fallback": None, "answer_status_ok": False, "answer_status_tool_error": True},
        {**base, "request_id": "d", "answer_grounded": None, "answer_fallback": None, "answer_status_ok": None, "answer_status_tool_error": None},
    ]
    stats = summarize(rows)["planner"]["similar"]["all"]
    assert stats["answer_grounded"] == 1.0 and stats["answer_fallback"] == 0.5
    assert stats["answer_status_ok"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3) and stats["answer_status_tool_error"] == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_eval_integration.py::test_run_planner_adds_the_answer_without_touching_the_recommendation_or_planner_usage`  (0.0244s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_run_planner_adds_the_answer_without_touching_the_recommendation_or_planner_usage(corpus):
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    plain = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), _two_items_request(), "planner", 2)

    def answerer(outcome):
        infer_llm = ScriptedLLM(good_reply(outcome.items))
        return compose_answer(outcome, infer_llm, mode="llm")

    with_answer = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), _two_items_request(), "planner", 2, answerer=answerer, inference_mode="llm")
    for key in ("recommended", "plan", "error", "reflections", "first_attempt_ok", "ask_user"):
        assert with_answer[key] == plain[key], key
    brief = lambda t: [(s["tool"], s["ok"], s["error"]) for s in t["steps"]]  # latency differs between two runs
    assert brief(with_answer) == brief(plain)
    assert {k: v for k, v in with_answer["usage"].items() if k != "latency_seconds"} == {k: v for k, v in plain["usage"].items() if k != "latency_seconds"}
    assert with_answer["recommended"] == [VG_A, VG_B]
    assert with_answer["answer_status"] == "ok" and with_answer["answer_composer"] == "llm" and with_answer["answer_grounded"] is True
    assert with_answer["answer_fallback"] is None and VG_A in with_answer["answer"] and with_answer["inference_mode"] == "llm"
    assert with_answer["inference_usage"]["attempts"] == 1 and with_answer["usage"]["attempts"] == 1  # two separate ledgers
    json.dumps(with_answer, ensure_ascii=False)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_run_type1_keeps_the_recommendation_and_adds_the_answer():
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="similar-warm-0", kind="similar", segment="warm", text="similar to x", user_id="u1", seed_item_id=VG_A, targets=[1])
    out = {"ok": True, "cold_start": False, "items": [{"rank": 1, "item_id": VG_B, "score": 0.4, "domain": "Video_Games", "title": "Beta", "price": 3.0}]}
    traj = script.run_type1(_FakeType1(out), request, 2, answerer=lambda o: compose_answer(o, None, mode="template"), inference_mode="template")
    assert traj["agent"] == "type1" and traj["recommended"] == [VG_B] and traj["error"] is None and traj["usage"] == {}
    assert traj["answer_status"] == "ok" and traj["answer_composer"] == "template" and traj["answer_fallback"] is None and VG_B in traj["answer"]
    failed = script.run_type1(_FakeType1({"ok": False, "stage": "rank", "error": "boom"}), request, 2,
                              answerer=lambda o: compose_answer(o, None, mode="template"), inference_mode="template")
    assert failed["error"] == "rank: boom" and failed["recommended"] == [] and failed["answer_status"] == "tool_error"
    bare = script.run_type1(_FakeType1(out), request, 2)
    assert bare["recommended"] == [VG_B] and bare["answer"] is None
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_eval_integration.py::test_resume_keeps_old_records_and_drops_other_inference_modes`  (0.0036s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_keeps_old_records_and_drops_other_inference_modes():
    keep = _load_run_agent_eval().is_resumable
    text_of = {"r0": "hello"}
    rec = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "planner", "text": "hello", "reflect_rounds": 0, "inference_mode": "llm"}
    assert keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "llm")
    assert not keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "template")  # LLM and template answers must not be mixed in one experiment
    old = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != "inference_mode"}  # written before Inference existed: reusable, answer columns stay empty
    assert keep(old, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "llm") and keep(old, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "template")
    type1 = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "type1", "text": "hello", "inference_mode": "llm"}
    assert keep(type1, text_of, ["type1"], 0, "llm") and not keep(type1, text_of, ["type1"], 0, "template")
    baseline = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "baseline", "text": "hello", "inference_mode": None}
    assert keep(baseline, text_of, ["baseline"], 0, "template")  # no Inference for the baseline
    assert keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0)  # the old call signature still works
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_inference_mode_comes_from_yaml_not_cli(monkeypatch, capsys, tmp_path):
    """inference.mode sống trong configs/agent/inference.yaml (hoặc CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH), không còn là CLI flag."""
    from package.config.load_config import ConfigError, load_config

    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", ["run_agent_eval.py", "--help"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as helped:
        script.main()
    assert helped.value.code == 0 and "--inference-mode" not in capsys.readouterr().out

    overlay = tmp_path / "overlay.yaml"
    overlay.write_text("agent/inference:\n  inference:\n    mode: gpt\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay))
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        load_config("agent/inference")
    monkeypatch.delenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH")
    assert load_config("agent/inference")["inference"]["mode"] in ("llm", "template")
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["llm", "template"])
def test_end_to_end_scenario(corpus, mode):
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="similar-warm-0", kind="similar", segment="warm", text=f"similar to {VG_A} and {VG_B}", targets=[1])
    from tests.agents.inference.helpers import item, good_reply

    test_items = [item(VG_A, "Game A"), item(VG_B, "Game B")]
    infer_llm = ScriptedLLM(good_reply(test_items) if mode == "llm" else "")

    def answerer(outcome):
        return compose_answer(outcome, infer_llm, mode=mode)

    traj = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), request, "planner", 2, answerer=answerer, inference_mode=mode)
    baseline = script.empty_trajectory(request, "baseline", [])
    baseline["recommended"] = [VG_A]
    rows = [score_trajectory(request, t, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2) for t in (traj, baseline)]
    summary = summarize(rows)

    planner = summary["planner"]["similar"]["all"]
    assert planner["task_success"] == 1.0 and planner["answer_status_ok"] == 1.0 and planner["answer_grounded"] == 1.0
    assert planner["answer_fallback"] == 0.0  # mode=template is a choice, not a fallback
    assert len(infer_llm.calls) == (1 if mode == "llm" else 0)
    assert planner["inference_attempts"] == (1 if mode == "llm" else 0)
    assert "answer_grounded" not in summary["baseline"]["similar"]["all"]  # the baseline has no Inference
    json.dumps(summary)
```

```python
def test_reference_plans_backward_compatibility():
    """Test that REFERENCE_PLANS dict-like accessor works."""
    # Should support __getitem__
    assert REFERENCE_PLANS["similar"] == (("SimilarTool",), ("RankTool",))

    # Should support iteration
    kinds = list(REFERENCE_PLANS.keys())
    assert "similar" in kinds
    assert "personalized" in kinds

    # Should support items()
    for kind, groups in REFERENCE_PLANS.items():
        assert isinstance(kind, str)
        assert isinstance(groups, tuple)
```

_Test that modifying reference_plans.yaml clears the cache._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_reference_plans_cache_invalidation(tmp_path):
    """
    Test that modifying reference_plans.yaml clears the cache.
    """
    # Clear cache to avoid interference
    _file_mtime_cache.clear()
    _cached_reference_plans.cache_clear()

    agent_dir = tmp_path / "configs" / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Create initial config
    (agent_dir / "reference_plans.yaml").write_text(
        "reference_plans:\n  similar:\n    - ['SimilarTool']\n    - ['RankTool']\n  personalized:\n    - ['MemoryTool']\n"
        "    - ['SearchTool', 'FilterTool']\n    - ['RankTool']\n  constrained:\n    - ['SearchTool', 'FilterTool']\n"
        "    - ['RankTool']\n  cold_text:\n    - ['SearchTool', 'FilterTool']\n    - ['RankTool']\n  ambiguous: []\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # Load with custom config dir
    from package.config.load_config import load_config
    cfg = load_config("agent/reference_plans", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg["reference_plans"]["similar"] == [["SimilarTool"], ["RankTool"]]

    # Wait to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify config
    (agent_dir / "reference_plans.yaml").write_text(
        "reference_plans:\n  similar:\n    - ['SearchTool']\n    - ['RankTool']\n  personalized:\n    - ['MemoryTool']\n"
        "    - ['SearchTool', 'FilterTool']\n    - ['RankTool']\n  constrained:\n    - ['SearchTool', 'FilterTool']\n"
        "    - ['RankTool']\n  cold_text:\n    - ['SearchTool', 'FilterTool']\n    - ['RankTool']\n  ambiguous: []\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # Reload should get new values
    cfg2 = load_config("agent/reference_plans", config_dir=tmp_path / "configs")
    assert cfg2["reference_plans"]["similar"] == [["SearchTool"], ["RankTool"]]
```

```python
def test_reference_plans_validation(tmp_path):
    """Test that invalid reference plans are rejected."""

    _file_mtime_cache.clear()

    agent_dir = tmp_path / "configs" / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Invalid config (not a dict)
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["baseline", "hybrid"])
def test_scorer_mode_comes_from_yaml_and_is_recorded(environment, monkeypatch, mode):
    base, paths, items = environment
    exp_dir = _launch(monkeypatch, base, mode, mode=mode)

    config = yaml.safe_load((exp_dir / "config.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["config"]
    assert config["scorer"]["mode"] == mode
    assert config["protocol"]["scorer"] == config["scorer"]
    assert config["scorer"]["history_splits"] == ["train"]
    assert config["knn_cache_used"] is False

    rows = [json.loads(s) for s in (exp_dir / "trajectories.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    type1 = [r for r in rows if r["agent"] == "type1"]
    assert type1 and all(r["steps"] and r["tool_calls"] for r in type1)

    if mode == "hybrid":
        train = pd.read_parquet(paths.split_path("train"))
        id2item = {v: k for k, v in items.items()}
        for request in (json.loads(s) for s in (exp_dir / "requests.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()):
            if request.get("user_idx") is None:
                continue
            seen = {id2item[i] for i in train.loc[train.user_idx == request["user_idx"], "item_idx"]}
            for row in rows:
                if row["agent"] == "baseline" and row["request_id"] == request["request_id"]:
                    assert not seen.intersection(row["recommended"])
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_scorer_yaml_cli.py::test_scorer_mode_comes_from_yaml_and_is_recorded[hybrid]`  (0.2147s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["baseline", "hybrid"])
def test_scorer_mode_comes_from_yaml_and_is_recorded(environment, monkeypatch, mode):
    base, paths, items = environment
    exp_dir = _launch(monkeypatch, base, mode, mode=mode)

    config = yaml.safe_load((exp_dir / "config.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["config"]
    assert config["scorer"]["mode"] == mode
    assert config["protocol"]["scorer"] == config["scorer"]
    assert config["scorer"]["history_splits"] == ["train"]
    assert config["knn_cache_used"] is False

    rows = [json.loads(s) for s in (exp_dir / "trajectories.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    type1 = [r for r in rows if r["agent"] == "type1"]
    assert type1 and all(r["steps"] and r["tool_calls"] for r in type1)

    if mode == "hybrid":
        train = pd.read_parquet(paths.split_path("train"))
        id2item = {v: k for k, v in items.items()}
        for request in (json.loads(s) for s in (exp_dir / "requests.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()):
            if request.get("user_idx") is None:
                continue
            seen = {id2item[i] for i in train.loc[train.user_idx == request["user_idx"], "item_idx"]}
            for row in rows:
                if row["agent"] == "baseline" and row["request_id"] == request["request_id"]:
                    assert not seen.intersection(row["recommended"])
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_type1_audit.py::test_type1_preserves_ordered_calls_and_failures_without_previous_request[None]`  (0.0045s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", [None, ToolInputError, RuntimeError])
def test_type1_preserves_ordered_calls_and_failures_without_previous_request(failure):
    logger = ToolCallLogger()
    retrieve = AuditTool("SimilarTool", {"candidates": [{"item_id": "D::a"}]}, logger)
    rank = AuditTool("RankTool", {"ranked": [{"item_id": "D::a", "score": 1}], "cold_start": False}, logger, failure)
    fetch = AuditTool("LookupTool", {"items": [{"item_id": "D::a", "title": "A"}]}, logger)
    runner = Type1Agent(retrieve, rank, fetch)
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="r", kind="similar", segment="warm", text="similar", user_id="u", seed_item_id="D::seed")
    for _ in range(2):
        row = run_type1(runner, request, 1)
        expected = ["SimilarTool", "RankTool"] + ([] if failure else ["LookupTool"])
        assert [call["tool"] for call in row["tool_calls"]] == expected
        assert [step["tool"] for step in row["steps"]] == expected
        assert all(c["agent"] == "type1" and c["input_hash"] and c["timestamp"] for c in row["tool_calls"])
        assert all(c["latency_seconds"] >= 0 for c in row["tool_calls"])
        assert all("data" in c for c in row["tool_calls"])
        assert all("data" in s for s in row["steps"])
        if failure:
            assert row["error"] and not row["steps"][-1]["ok"]
        else:
            assert row["recommended"] == ["D::a"]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_type1_audit.py::test_type1_preserves_ordered_calls_and_failures_without_previous_request[ToolInputError]`  (0.0024s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", [None, ToolInputError, RuntimeError])
def test_type1_preserves_ordered_calls_and_failures_without_previous_request(failure):
    logger = ToolCallLogger()
    retrieve = AuditTool("SimilarTool", {"candidates": [{"item_id": "D::a"}]}, logger)
    rank = AuditTool("RankTool", {"ranked": [{"item_id": "D::a", "score": 1}], "cold_start": False}, logger, failure)
    fetch = AuditTool("LookupTool", {"items": [{"item_id": "D::a", "title": "A"}]}, logger)
    runner = Type1Agent(retrieve, rank, fetch)
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="r", kind="similar", segment="warm", text="similar", user_id="u", seed_item_id="D::seed")
    for _ in range(2):
        row = run_type1(runner, request, 1)
        expected = ["SimilarTool", "RankTool"] + ([] if failure else ["LookupTool"])
        assert [call["tool"] for call in row["tool_calls"]] == expected
        assert [step["tool"] for step in row["steps"]] == expected
        assert all(c["agent"] == "type1" and c["input_hash"] and c["timestamp"] for c in row["tool_calls"])
        assert all(c["latency_seconds"] >= 0 for c in row["tool_calls"])
        assert all("data" in c for c in row["tool_calls"])
        assert all("data" in s for s in row["steps"])
        if failure:
            assert row["error"] and not row["steps"][-1]["ok"]
        else:
            assert row["recommended"] == ["D::a"]
```

```python
def test_env_interpolation_default_and_shell_semantics(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    (tmp_path / "demo.yaml").write_text("a: ${DEMO_KEY:-}\nb: ${DEMO_KEY:-fallback}\nc: x-${DEMO_KEY:-y}-z\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.delenv("DEMO_KEY", raising=False)
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path) == {"a": "", "b": "fallback", "c": "x-y-z"}
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_KEY", "")  # set-but-empty counts as unset for ":-", like the shell
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path)["b"] == "fallback"
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_KEY", "real")
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path) == {"a": "real", "b": "real", "c": "x-real-z"}
```

```python
def test_llm_yaml_holds_no_literal_credential(monkeypatch):
    raw = yaml.safe_load((CONFIG_DIR / "agent/llm.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))  # un-interpolated, as committed
    assert re.fullmatch(r"\$\{[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(:-[^}]*)?\}", raw["api_key"] or "${EMPTY:-}"), (
        "api_key must be an env reference like ${LLM_API_KEY:-}, never a literal secret"
    )
    monkeypatch.delenv("LLM_API_KEY", raising=False)
    assert load_config("agent/llm")["api_key"] == ""  # no secret required to load the config (local servers, CI)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_API_KEY", "from-env")
    assert load_config("agent/llm")["api_key"] == "from-env"
```

```python
def test_every_data_paths_dir_is_distinct_and_project():
    paths = get_data_paths()
    dirs = [getattr(paths, f.name) for f in dataclasses.fields(paths) if f.name != "tag"]
    assert len(set(dirs)) == len(dirs)
    # All directories should be under the project root (or be the project root itself)
    # src_root is the project root ("."); other dirs should have it as ancestor
    for d in dirs:
        if d == paths.src_root:
            # src_root IS the project root
            continue
        assert paths.src_root in d.parents or paths.src_root == d
```

_Test that modifying a config file clears the LRU cache in _limits.py._

```python
def test_config_cache_invalidation_on_file_change(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Test that modifying a config file clears the LRU cache in _limits.py."""
    (tmp_path / "test_config.yaml").write_text("test:\n  value: 10\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # First load
    cfg1 = load_config("test_config", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg1["test"]["value"] == 10

    # Wait a bit to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify the file
    (tmp_path / "test_config.yaml").write_text("test:\n  value: 20\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # Second load should get the new value
    cfg2 = load_config("test_config", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg2["test"]["value"] == 20
```

```python
def test_config_schema_validation(tmp_path):
    """Test that Pydantic schema validation works for agent configs."""
    # Create agent subdirectory
    agent_dir = tmp_path / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir()

    # Valid config
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    cfg = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg["agent"]["max_steps"] == 8

    # Invalid config (negative value)
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: -1\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="Config validation failed"):
        load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path)
```

```python
def test_judge_config_real_file_loads():
    """The committed configs/eval/judge.yaml must pass its schema — guards the
    Jev evaluation path (evaluate_product_reply_with_jev -> load_config('eval/judge'))."""
    cfg = load_config("eval/judge")

    assert cfg["provider"] == "jev"
    assert cfg["model"] == "jev-latest"
    assert cfg["timeout_seconds"] == 30.0
    assert cfg["enable_long_context"] is False
    assert 0.0 <= cfg["evaluation"]["faithfulness"]["support_threshold"] <= 1.0
    criteria = cfg["evaluation"]["communication"]["criteria"]
    assert set(criteria) == {"relevance", "clarity", "language", "honesty_about_gaps"}
    for criterion in criteria.values():
        assert criterion["instructions"]
        assert len(criterion["rubric"]) == 5
```

```python
def test_judge_config_rejects_extra_and_missing_keys(tmp_path):
    """extra=forbid: unknown keys fail loudly instead of being silently ignored."""
    eval_dir = tmp_path / "eval"
    eval_dir.mkdir()

    base = (
        "provider: jev\nmodel: jev-latest\nbase_url: https://api.typesafe.ai\n"
        "timeout_seconds: 30.0\n"
        "evaluation:\n  faithfulness:\n    support_threshold: 0.7\n    instructions: x\n"
        "  communication:\n    criteria:\n      relevance:\n        instructions: x\n        rubric: [a, b, c, d, e]\n"
    )
    (eval_dir / "judge.yaml").write_text(base + "bogus_key: 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="Config validation failed"):
        load_config("eval/judge", config_dir=tmp_path)

    (eval_dir / "judge.yaml").write_text(base, encoding="utf-8")
    cfg = load_config("eval/judge", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg["evaluation"]["faithfulness"]["support_threshold"] == 0.7
    assert "bogus_key" not in cfg
```

```python
def test_limits_refresh_on_env_set_then_getter(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Setting CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH then calling a limits getter directly must not be stale."""
    base = get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds
    overlay = _write_overlay(tmp_path, {

    _use_overlay(monkeypatch, overlay)

    assert get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds == base
```
